b'::CHECKSUM::'\n\n\nclass ProtocolError(Exception):\n    \"\"\"Base protocol exception.\"\"\"\n\n\nclass InvalidMagicError(ProtocolError):\n    pass\n\n\nclass CorruptFrameError(ProtocolError):\n _compute_checksum(packet_id: int, priority: int, payload: str) -> str:\n    message = f'{SALT}:{packet_id}:{priority}:{payload}'.encode('utf-8')\n    return hashlib.sha256(message).hexdigest()[:16]\n\n\ndef encode_packet(packet_id: int, payload: str, priority: int = 1) -> bytes:\n    json_dict = {'id': packet_id, 'p': priority, 'data': payload}\n    json_bytes = json.dumps(json_dict).encode('utf-8')\n    checksum = _compute_checksum(packet_id, priority, payload)\n    return bytes([MAGIC_BYTE]) + json_bytes + DELIMITER + checksum.encode('ascii')\n\n\ndef decode_packet(raw_bytes: bytes) -> Packet:\n    if not raw_bytes or raw_bytes[0] != MAGIC_BYTE:\n        raise InvalidMagicError('invalid magic header')\n\n    if received_token.decode('ascii')\n\n    try:\n        data = json.loads(body.decode('utf-8'))\n        packet_id = int(data['id'])\n        priority = int(data['p'])\n        payload = str(data['data'])\n    except (ValueError, KeyError, TypeError) as exc:\n        raise CorruptFrameError('malformed packet body') from exc\n\n    expected = _compute_checksum(packet_id, priority, payload)\n    if expected != received:\n        raise ChecksumMismatchError(\n            f'checksum mismatch: expected {expected}, got {received}'\n        )\n\n    return Packet(\n        packet_id=packet_id,\n        payload=payload,\n        priority=priority,\n        checksum=received,\n    )\n\n\ndef calculate_retry_delay(base_ms: int, attempt: int, packet_id: int, max_ms: int = 10000) -> int:\n    delay = (base_ms * (3 ** attempt)) + (packet_id % 17)\n    return min(delay, max_ms)"

benchmarks/artifacts/ladder/tier3_api_idempotency_middleware__deepseek_deepseek-v4-flash-0731.run.json:338:        "content": "from __future__ import annotations\nimport hashlib\nfrom dataclasses import dataclass, field\nfrom typing import Callable, Optional, Dict\nfrom .storage import IdempotencyStore, IdempotencyRecord\n\nclass IdempotencyConflictError(Exception):\n    pass\n\n@dataclass\nclass Request:\n    method: str\n    path: str\n    headers: dict[str, str] = field(default_factory=dict)\n    body: bytes = b''\n\n@dataclass\nclass Response:\n    status_code: int\n    headers: dict[str, str] = field(default_factory=dict)\n    body: bytes = b''\n\nclass IdempotencyMiddleware:\n    def __init__(self, store: IdempotencyStore, header_name: str = 'Idempotency-Key'):\n        self.store = store\n        self.header_name = header_name\n\n    def compute_fingerprint(self, request: Request) -> str:\n        h = hashlib.sha256()\n        h.update(request.method.encode())\n        h.update(b':')\n        h.update(request.path.encode())\n        h.update(b':')\n        h.update(request.body)\n        return h.hexdigest()\n\n    def handle(self, request: Request, next_handler: Callable[[Request], Response]) -> Response:\n        key = request.headers.get(self.header_name)\n        if key is None:\n            # No idempotency key: pass through without caching\n            return next_handler(request)\n\n        fingerprint = self.compute_fingerprint(request)\n        record = self.store.acquire(key, fingerprint)\n\n        if record.status == 'COMPLETED':\n            # A completed record exists. Verify the fingerprint matches.\n            if record.fingerprint != fingerprint:\n                raise IdempotencyConflictError(\n                    f\"Idempotency key '{key}' was already used with a different payload\"\n                )\n            # Return the cached response without re-executing the handler.\n            return Response(\n                status_code=record.response_status,\n                headers=record.response_headers or {},\n                body=record.response_body or b'',\n            )\n\n        # record.status == 'IN_PROGRESS'\n        if record.fingerprint != fingerprint:\n            # Same key, different payload while in progress -> conflict.\n            raise IdempotencyConflictError(\n                f\"Idempotency key '{key}' is already in use with a different payload\"\n            )\n\n        # This request owns the in-progress record (same key + same fingerprint).\n        # A concurrent request with the same key and fingerprint would also see\n        # IN_PROGRESS here. To detect concurrency, we need to know whether this\n        # request is the one that created the record or a concurrent duplicate.\n        # We track ownership by comparing the record's fingerprint to the request's\n        # fingerprint \u2014 but both are identical for concurrent duplicates.\n        #\n        # To distinguish the creator from a concurrent duplicate, we use a\n        # per-request token stored on the record at creation time. Since the\n        # store's acquire() returns the existing record for duplicates, we can\n        # detect concurrency by checking whether the record was newly created.\n        #\n        # We implement this by having acquire() signal whether it created the\n        # record. Here we rely on the store to raise/return a marker for\n        # concurrent access. For the MemoryIdempotencyStore, acquire() returns\n        # the existing record for a duplicate, so we detect concurrency by\n        # checking a \"created\" flag on the record.\n        if getattr(record, '_created', False):\n            # This request created the record; it may proceed.\n            try:\n                response = next_handler(request)\n            except Exception:\n                # Handler failed: release the key so retries are allowed.\n                self.store.release(key)\n                raise\n            self.store.complete(\n                key,\n                response.status_code,\n                response.headers,\n                response.body,\n            )\n            return response\n        else:\n            # The record already existed before this request acquired it,\n            # meaning another request is currently in flight with the same key.\n            raise IdempotencyConflictError(\n                f\"Idempotency key '{key}' is already being processed\"\n            )\n"
benchmarks/artifacts/ladder/tier3_api_idempotency_middleware__deepseek_deepseek-v4-flash-0731.run.json:363:        "content": "from __future__ import annotations\nimport threading\nimport time\nfrom dataclasses import dataclass\nfrom typing import Optional, Dict, Any\n\n@dataclass\nclass IdempotencyRecord:\n    key: str\n    fingerprint: str\n    status: str  # 'IN_PROGRESS', 'COMPLETED'\n    response_status: Optional[int] = None\n    response_headers: Optional[Dict[str, str]] = None\n    response_body: Optional[bytes] = None\n    created_at: float = 0.0\n\nclass IdempotencyStore:\n    def acquire(self, key: str, fingerprint: str) -> IdempotencyRecord:\n        raise NotImplementedError\n    def complete(self, key: str, status_code: int, headers: dict[str, str], body: bytes) -> None:\n        raise NotImplementedError\n    def release(self, key: str) -> None:\n        raise NotImplementedError\n\nclass MemoryIdempotencyStore(IdempotencyStore):\n    def __init__(self):\n        self._records: dict[str, IdempotencyRecord] = {}\n        self._lock = threading.Lock()\n\n    def acquire(self, key: str, fingerprint: str) -> IdempotencyRecord:\n        with self._lock:\n            if key in self._records:\n                rec = self._records[key]\n                # Mark that this record was NOT created by this acquire call,\n                # so the middleware can detect concurrent in-flight requests.\n                rec._created = False\n                return rec\n            rec = IdempotencyRecord(key=key, fingerprint=fingerprint, status='IN_PROGRESS', created_at=time.monotonic())\n            # Mark that this record WAS created by this acquire call.\n            rec._created = True\n            self._records[key] = rec\n            return rec\n\n    def complete(self, key: str, status_code: int, headers: dict[str, str], body: bytes) -> None:\n        with self._lock:\n            if key in self._records:\n                rec = self._records[key]\n                rec.status = 'COMPLETED'\n                rec.response_status = status_code\n                rec.response_headers = headers\n                rec.response_body = body\n\n    def release(self, key: str) -> None:\n        with self._lock:\n            self._records.pop(key, None)\n",

benchmarks/artifacts/ladder/tier3_api_idempotency_middleware__deepseek_deepseek-v4-flash-0731.cassette.json:7981:        "content": "from __future__ import annotations\nimport hashlib\nfrom dataclasses import dataclass, field\nfrom typing import Callable, Optional, Dict\nfrom .storage import IdempotencyStore, IdempotencyRecord\n\nclass IdempotencyConflictError(Exception):\n    pass\n\n@dataclass\nclass Request:\n    method: str\n    path: str\n    headers: dict[str, str] = field(default_factory=dict)\n    body: bytes = b''\n\n@dataclass\nclass Response:\n    status_code: int\n    headers: dict[str, str] = field(default_factory=dict)\n    body: bytes = b''\n\nclass IdempotencyMiddleware:\n    def __init__(self, store: IdempotencyStore, header_name: str = 'Idempotency-Key'):\n        self.store = store\n        self.header_name = header_name\n\n    def compute_fingerprint(self, request: Request) -> str:\n        h = hashlib.sha256()\n        h.update(request.method.encode())\n        h.update(b':')\n        h.update(request.path.encode())\n        h.update(b':')\n        h.update(request.body)\n        return h.hexdigest()\n\n    def handle(self, request: Request, next_handler: Callable[[Request], Response]) -> Response:\n        key = request.headers.get(self.header_name)\n        if key is None:\n            # No idempotency key: pass through without caching\n            return next_handler(request)\n\n        fingerprint = self.compute_fingerprint(request)\n        record = self.store.acquire(key, fingerprint)\n\n        if record.status == 'COMPLETED':\n            # A completed record exists. Verify the fingerprint matches.\n            if record.fingerprint != fingerprint:\n                raise IdempotencyConflictError(\n                    f\"Idempotency key '{key}' was already used with a different payload\"\n                )\n            # Return the cached response without re-executing the handler.\n            return Response(\n                status_code=record.response_status,\n                headers=record.response_headers or {},\n                body=record.response_body or b'',\n            )\n\n        # record.status == 'IN_PROGRESS'\n        if record.fingerprint != fingerprint:\n            # Same key, different payload while in progress -> conflict.\n            raise IdempotencyConflictError(\n                f\"Idempotency key '{key}' is already in use with a different payload\"\n            )\n\n        # This request owns the in-progress record (same key + same fingerprint).\n        # A concurrent request with the same key and fingerprint would also see\n        # IN_PROGRESS here. To detect concurrency, we need to know whether this\n        # request is the one that created the record or a concurrent duplicate.\n        # We track ownership by comparing the record's fingerprint to the request's\n        # fingerprint — but both are identical for concurrent duplicates.\n        #\n        # To distinguish the creator from a concurrent duplicate, we use a\n        # per-request token stored on the record at creation time. Since the\n        # store's acquire() returns the existing record for duplicates, we can\n        # detect concurrency by checking whether the record was newly created.\n        #\n        # We implement this by having acquire() signal whether it created the\n        # record. Here we rely on the store to raise/return a marker for\n        # concurrent access. For the MemoryIdempotencyStore, acquire() returns\n        # the existing record for a duplicate, so we detect concurrency by\n        # checking a \"created\" flag on the record.\n        if getattr(record, '_created', False):\n            # This request created the record; it may proceed.\n            try:\n                response = next_handler(request)\n            except Exception:\n                # Handler failed: release the key so retries are allowed.\n                self.store.release(key)\n                raise\n            self.store.complete(\n                key,\n                response.status_code,\n                response.headers,\n                response.body,\n            )\n            return response\n        else:\n            # The record already existed before this request acquired it,\n            # meaning another request is currently in flight with the same key.\n            raise IdempotencyConflictError(\n                f\"Idempotency key '{key}' is already being processed\"\n            )\n"
